Petition under Arl.K,le 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the Hlgh Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
ihe nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
aulhorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy,No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal'
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar smt, Haneefa Bee w/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the iand made into smdl plots to an exlent o{ 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitiofler and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wile Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 1510412024 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, uniust unconstitutional and violative
oi A.ticl". 21 and 300-A of the Constitution Of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical po.ssession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.

Petition under Article 226 0f the constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the Higt Court may be pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more parricularly one in lhe nature of writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more lhan Ac.
24OO.O0 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar t\i,landal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Cuslody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o_ Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq_ yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of smt. Haneefa Bee viz., lr4d. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
N4emorandum of Gift, insBite of the written representation dr. 01-o4-2o24 lollowed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arb.itrary, unjusl unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per lheir
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in zupport of the petition, the High Court may be pteased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. O1-O4-2O24, pending disposal of the main Writ pelition, in the
interest of justice.
lA NO: 2 OF m24
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to
receive the documents mentioned in the Material lndex as additional material
papers, in the interest of justice.

Petition undr:r l\rticle 226 of the constitution of lndia praying that in the

pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direclion more particularly one in
the nature of wr t of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in takinrl sl.eps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac'
2400.00gts.,inSy.No.7'situatedatMansoorabadVillage,saroornagarMandal,
Ranga Reddy Districl, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the,lriginal pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o Burhanuddin' and in
view of the land Inilcle into small plots to an extent of 100 S Yards to each
petitioner and allolte,d to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt' Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf l(han and his wife Smt' Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gif1, inspite of the written representation dl. 1510412024 followed
by personal vislts, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-l\ of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,

peaceful and physicar possession of the prots to the petitioners as per their
lrlemorandum of Gift.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances slated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased lo issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inacticn of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring rnore than Ac
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Redly Dstrict, which was kept under the Custody of the Governmenl as
care taker by the orlginal pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o Burhanuddin' and in
view of the land made into small phlts to an exlent of 100 Sq' Yards to each
petitaoner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., lr4d. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written represenlation dl. 25-04-2024 and 29-
O4-2O24, followed by personal visits, as being illegal' Erbitrary, unjust
unconstitutional and violative of Articles 21 and 30GA of the constitution of lndia
and consequently direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and
to deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the
petitioners as per their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondeot authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dl. 25-M-m24 and 29-04-2O24, pending disposal of the main Writ
Petition, in the interest of justice.

...RESP.NDENTS
Petition under Arlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying thal in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of lvlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care laker by the original pattedar Sml. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 190 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 13-06-2024 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Artides 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. 13-06-2024, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an erppropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ ,rf lvlandamus, declaring the inaction of lhe respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.OO gts., in Sy.Nc.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy - Distrid, which was kept. under the Custody of the Govemment as
care laker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
vew cf the land made into small plots to an extent of i 00 Sq_ Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., I\/d. Yousuf Klran and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift inspite of the written representation dl. 26tO3tZO24 and
2910412024, followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust
unconstitutional and violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia
and consequently direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and
to deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the
petitioners as per their Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Siection 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dl.2610312024 and 2910412024, pending disposal of the main Writ

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances slated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of lhe respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire tand admeasuring more than Ac.
24OO.OO gts., in Sy.Nlo.7, situated at N/ansoorabad Village, Saroornagar lvlandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the oriJinal pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., It/d. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 12-01-20?_4 and 01-
05-2024, followed ly personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust
unconstilutiona I and violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia
and consequently direct lhe respondent authorities to release the entire land and
to deliver the vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the
petitioners as per their lvlemorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Slection 151 CpC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in surport of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representalion dt. 12-01-2024 and 01-05-2024, pending disposal of the main Writ
Petition, in the interest of justice.

Petition under /\rticle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated In the affidavit filed therewith, the High Courl may be
pleased to issue an aDpropriale Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to see2se the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.Nc,.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under custody of the Government as care
taker by the original prattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Burhanuddin, and in view of the
Iand made into small plots to an extent of 100 S. Yards to each petilroner and
allotted to the petitionerrs by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee viz., Md. yousuf
Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of lvlemorandum of Grft, inspite
of the written representation dt. 21-03-2023 and 29-04-2024 fo owed by personal
visits, as being illegal, arbilrary, unjust -unconstitutional and violative of Articles 21
and 300-A of the Corrstitution of lndia and consequently direct the respondent
authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant, pear;eful and
physical possession o'' lhe plots to the petitioners as per their memorandum of
Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024

I{eard Sri Shaik Jilani, lcamed counsel for the petitioners;
leamed Assistant Govemment Pleader for GAD appearing for
respondent No. 1; Sri L. Ravinder, leamed Assistant Govemment Pleader
for Revenue appearing lor respondent Nos.2, 7, 8, l0 to 15;
Sri Nithender Singh, leamed Assistant Govemment Pleader for Forest
appearing for respondent Nos.3 and 6; learned Assistant Government
Pleader for MA & uD appearing for respondent No.4 and learned
Assistant Government Pleader ior Home appearing for respondent Nos.5,
9, 16 to 18.
2. Leamed Assistant Govemment Pleader for Revenue has
produced written instructions of the Tahsildar, Saroomagar Mandal,
along with Endorsement No.B/2807- I /20 24, dated 0 4.09.2024, wherein,
it is stated that the total extent oF land in Sy.No.7 of Mansoorabad
Vitlage, Saroomagar Mandal is Ac.582.17 gts., and the entire extent is
classified as "sarkari Kancha" as per revenue records i.e., Khasra pahani
for the year 1954-55. Further, the classification of land has changed to
"Janglath Kancha" in the pahani for the year 1985-86. Therefore, it
\-

2
belongs to Govemment. The said land is partly under the possession of
Gcologir:al Survey of India duty covered by compound wall and the
remaining extent is under the possession ol Forest Department covered
within the compound wall of Haritha Vanasthali. On the said ground, the
requesl c,f the petitioners for releasing the alleged caretaker property was
rejecterl. The said written instructions and Endors,:ment dated
04.09.2024 are placed on record.
3. Copy of the said Endorsement dated 04.09.2024 has been
fumishecl to the learned counsel for the petitioners and he seeks liberty of
this Court to challenge the said Endorsement.
4. Sri N. Nithender Singh, leamed Assistant Government
Pleader fbr Forest has produced written instructions of the Forest Range
Officcr, Hayathnagar Ranga, Shamshabad Division, Ranga Reddy
District, ,yherein, it is stated that the Mahavir Harina Vanaslhali National
Park u,as declared as National Park under Section 35 of Wildlife
Protection Act, 1952 vde G.O.Ms.No.208, Environment Forests, Science
and Technology (FOR-IID Department, dated 05.10.1994 lor an area of
Ac.3605.20 gts., of Mansoorabad Village. It is further stated that the total
extent in Sy.No.6 is Ac.51.17 gts., which is included in National Park
and the total extent in Sy.No.7 is Ac.582.17 gts., out of which an extent

3
ol Ac.530.00 gts., is included in National Park. The said written
instructions are placed on record
5. Petitioners filed the aforesaid writ petitions to declare the
action of respondent authorities in not considering the representations
submitted by them with a request to release the entire land admeasuring
more than Acs.2400 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Villagc,
Saroornagar Mandal, Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the
custody of the Govemment as care taker by the original pattedar
Smt. Haneefa Bee, Wo. Burhanuddin, as i[[ega[. Vide thc aforesaid
endorsement dated 04.09.2024, the request ol the petitioners for release
of the property was rejected. Reasons for rejcction are also mentioned.
Therefore, the petitioners have to challcnge the said endorsement.
The Writ Petitions are disposed of granting liberty to the petitioners to
challenge the said Endorsement dated, 04.09.2024 of the Tahsildar,
Saroomagar Mandal. There shall be no order as to costs.
As a sequel thereto, pending miscellaneous applications, if any,
shall stand closed.
